independence（multi-temp diversity, exp-055: Eff-K 从 2.0→2.11 但 delta 仍为 0）和 constraint quality（discriminative filtering）。这证明 bottleneck 不是简单的 diversity 或 quality 问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。\\n\\n**Key evidence**:\\n- **Condorcet independence quantification (统一脚本, 公式: Eff-K=K/(1+(K-1)κ))**: Mistral-7B FOLIO κ≈0.46 (Eff-K≈2.0, 三 seed 高度稳定), Mistral-7B PW-600 κ=0.200 (Eff-K=3.76), Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO κ=0.744 (Eff-K=1.31), Qwen2.5-14B LogiQA κ=0.821 (Eff-K=1.20), Qwen3-14B κ=0.773 (Eff-K=1.26), Qwen2.5-7B κ=0.658 (Eff-K=1.46). 跨模型清晰: Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.5→0 gain, Mistral FOLIO Eff-K≈2.0→variable gain, Mistral PW Eff-K=3.76 but SC too low→negative. κ stable across seeds (0.457-0.460), delta varies → seed-level constraint quality dominates.\\n- **Oracle control (exp-031)**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = 89.71% vs SC 75.49% (+14.22pp, p=2.0e-7). Unknown-type +31.9pp.\\n- **Two-layer constraint analysis**: 43% trivially_satisfied (taxonomy: satisfied by ALL answers) vs ~8% non-discriminative (filtering: same MAX-SAT score). Difference: trivially_satisfied constraints can still have different satisfaction DEGREES. Removing the 8% yields 0 gains → problem is not quality but confirmation bias direction.\\n- **K sensitivity (inverted U)**: K=4(-1.47pp), K=8(-0.98pp), K=12(+4.90pp p=0.013), K=16(+1.47pp), K=20(pending). Peak at K=12, then decline. Non-monotonic. K=4 SC ≈ K=12 SC but SICA differs by 6.4pp — proves SICA extracts different information than SC.\\n- **T sensitivity (inverted U, 3/4 points)**: T=0.3(-0.49pp p=1.0, exp-057 ✅), T=0.5(pending exp-058), T=0.7(+4.90pp p=0.013), T=1.0(+0.49pp p=0.739). 倒 U 型与 K sensitivity 平行：T 太低→traces 太一致(low diversity)，T 太高→traces 太 noisy。T=0.7 是 sweet spot。\\n- **Regime boundary**: Qwen-14B (SC=75.49%) Δ=+0.25pp vs Mistral-7B (SC=54.41%) Δ=+4.90pp p=0.013\\n- **Type C failure (exp-046)**: Mistral PW-D5 SC=39.33%, Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.010, κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63, contradiction rate 5.5%. Highest Eff-K yet worst delta → independence alone insufficient when SC too low.\\n- **Seed replication**: 2/3 seeds significant at α=0.05 (exp-033 p=0.013, exp-052 p=0.039), 1/3 null (exp-053). Effect IS real but fragile — motivates deep analysis of WHY.\\n- **Nine remediation failures (ALL CONFIRMED)**: contrastive prompting, cross-model, generational scaling, solver-guided, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT, discriminative filtering (0 gains), T=1.0 (delta collapse +4.90→+0.49pp), multi-temp (delta=+0.00pp, b=5 c=5). All 9 failed → trap is fundamental, bottleneck is structural.\\n- **Entropy mechanism**: High-entropy problems proportion: Mistral 35.8% vs Qwen 10.3%. Unanimous rate: 18.6% vs 58.8%.\\n- **Confirmation bias quantification**: Override precision 70.6% (Mistral) vs 28.6% (Qwen). Constraint agreement >80%: 31.9% vs 62.3%.\\n\\n## Story Spine (D059 Updated)\\n\\n- **Problem**: LLMs' reasoning can be improved by sampling multiple chains-of-thought and aggregating via majority voting (self-consistency). Recent work proposes symbolic augmentation: extract formal constraints from traces and use SAT solvers for answer selection. But does symbolic aggregation actually outperform simple voting?\\n- **Theoretical lens**: Condorcet Jury Theorem requires voter independence. We quantify trace independence via Fleiss' κ and show Effective-K << nominal K. This explains the fundamental limitation of same-model trace aggregation.\\n- **Systematic evaluation**: SICA (MAX-SAT over FOL constraints) across 3 domains × 5 model families. Oracle control separates extraction vs aggregation failure. Discriminative filtering reveals ~92% constraints are discriminative, yet SICA gains are fragile — confirmation bias, not constraint quality, is the bottleneck.\\n- **Regime characterization**: Three failure modes (Type A/B/C) + narrow Goldilocks zone. C0+SC as deployment diagnostic.\\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 targeted interventions (prompting, architecture, capability, verification, diversity, filtering) all fail to close the oracle gap, proving the bottleneck is fundamental to same-model self-extraction.\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"Across 5 model families, symbolic consensus aggregation shows a real but fragile effect: significant improvement in 2/3 seeds under specific conditions (Mistral-7B, FOLIO, T=0.7, K=12), but zero or negative gains under any perturbation. Condorcet analysis reveals the root cause: Effective-K = 1.3-1.8 for nominal K=12 due to trace non-independence. Oracle constraints prove the aggregation mechanism works (+14.22pp), but 9 remediation strategies all fail to overcome the self-extraction bottleneck. C0+SC provides a zero-cost deployment diagnostic.\\\"\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Defensive Experiments\\n\\n1. **\\\"Gains not statistically significant\\\"** → exp-033 p=0.013 (n=204), exp-031 oracle p=2.0e-7. Combined N>1,200.\\n2. **\\\"Only one positive result (exp-033)\\\"** → Seed replication: 2/3 significant (p=0.013, p=0.039). Effect IS real but fragile — the fragility itself IS the finding (K/T sensitivity, Condorcet independence).\\n3. **\\\"Condorcet framework is trivial\\\"** → Fleiss' κ quantification is novel in SC/SICA context. Eff-K is actionable: tells practitioners how many truly independent traces they get.\\n4. **\\\"Multi-temp / filtering should work\\\"** → We tried both. Multi-temp (exp-055): negative. Disc. filtering: 0 gains. T=1.0: delta collapse. These failures STRENGTHEN the structural bottleneck thesis.\\n5. **\\\"Only Qwen models\\\"** → 5 families: Qwen-14B, Mistral-7B, LLaMA-8B, DeepSeek-R1-7B, QwQ-32B.\\n6. **\\\"Only logic domain\\\"** → FOLIO + ProofWriter + LogiQA + StrategyQA + ARC-Challenge. Type B (domain formalizability) is itself a finding.\\n7. **\\\"Oracle is unfair comparison\\\"** → Diagnostic tool, not competing method. Separates extraction from aggregation.\\n8. **\\\"MAX-SAT is over-engineering\\\"** → exp-013: MaxSAT ≈ count-only. This IS the independence bottleneck evidence.\\n9. **\\\"Self-extraction confound\\\"** → Oracle control resolves completely.\\n10. **\\\"Published methods achieve gains (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer)\\\"** → Different paradigm (external verifiers/trained extractors). We characterize same-model self-extraction.\\n11. **\\\"C0 is trivially derived\\\"** → Zero-cost early stopping. Practical value.\\n12. **\\\"Why not just use a reasoning model?\\\"** → QwQ-32B: C0<5%, SICA≡SC.\\n13. **\\\"Random tiebreak baseline\\\"** → exp-003: SICA tiebreak 80% vs random 50%.\\n14. **\\\"Method improvement is incremental\\\"** → Grounded in diagnosis (independence + taxonomy), not ad-hoc.\\n15. **\\\"Weighted SC is simpler\\\"** → exp-016 planned. Weighted SC doesn't address independence.\\n16. **\\\"Within-model ICL transition contradicts regime theory\\\"** → exp-050b (SC=57.79%, Δ=-3.52pp): ICL changes trace generation characteristics, not just SC level. Paper must distinguish \\\"same model, different prompt\\\" from \\\"same model, same prompt, different accuracy.\\\"\\n\\n## Working Title\\n**\\\"Beyond Self-Consistency: How Trace Independence Limits Symbolic Consensus in LLM Reasoning\\\"**\\n(Alternatives:\\n- \\\"The Independence Bottleneck: Why Self-Extracted Symbolic Constraints Cannot Beat Majority Voting\\\"\\n- \\\"Symbolic Consensus Under the Condorcet Lens: Independence, Extraction, and the Limits of Self-Aggregation\\\")\\n\\nNOTE (Dream D037): Title MUST be scoped to \\\"self-extracted constraints\\\" / \\\"SICA\\\". Published methods (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer) use different approaches.\\n\\n## Paper Structure (EMNLP 8pp + appendix)\\n\\n### Main Tables\\n- T1: Main Results — models × domains, SC% / SICA% / Δpp / p-value, regime boundary\\n- T2: Oracle Control — Gold-FOL+MAX-SAT vs SC vs SICA-self, causal separation\\n- T3: Independence Quantification — Fleiss' κ / Eff-K / trace agreement per model\\n- T4: Extraction Error Taxonomy — trivially_satisfied / discriminative / both_wrong rates\\n- T5: K Sensitivity — K=4/8/12/16/20 with Eff-K and diversity metrics (inverted U curve)\\n\\n### Main Figures\\n- F1: Method Pipeline (SICA + oracle control design)\\n- F2: SC Accuracy vs SICA Δ scatter (hero figure, regime boundary)\\n- F3: K Sensitivity with diversity metrics (K vs Δ, K vs Eff-K)\\n- F4: Extraction error breakdown (stacked bar)\\n\\n### Appendix\\n- K ablation details, temperature sensitivity, per-domain results, overhead analysis\\n- Entropy-conditioned analysis (continuous scatter + bin sensitivity)\\n- Six remediation strategy details\\n- Confirmation bias quantification\\n- Seed replication analysis\\n- QwQ-32B / DeepSeek-R1 negative control\\n\\n## Claim Risk Notes (D059)\\n- **Seed replication**: 2/3 significant (p=0.013, p=0.039), 1/3 null. Frame as \\\"real but fragile effect\\\" — motivates analysis of WHY.\\n- **Method claims DEAD (D060)**: All three method improvements failed. Paper is pure analysis. 9 remediation failures are EVIDENCE, not limitation.\\n- **Condorcet novelty**: ✅ VERIFIED. arXiv:2409.00094 applies CJT to cross-model LLM ensembles (different angle). No prior work applies Fleiss' κ or Eff-K to within-model SC traces. Our framing is novel.\\n- **exp-050b complication**: ICL transition Δ=-3.52pp at SC=57.79% complicates simple \\\"SC determines SICA.\\\" Explain via trace generation characteristic change.\\n- **Variable naming inconsistency**: 56.4% cross-trace pairs share zero Z3 variables. Key mechanism evidence.\\n\\n## Required Citations (minimum 30 references)\\n\\n- **SC & extensions**: Wang et al. 2023 (SC), Chen et al. 2023 (USC), Loo et al. 2025 (SC scaling plateaus), Taubenfeld et al. 2025 (weighted SC), DiVeRSe (Li et al. 2023)\\n- **Neuro-symbolic reasoning**: DyLoS (EACL 2026), Aristotle (ACL 2025), FoVer (TACL 2025), LogicAgent (2025), Logic-LM (Pan et al. 2023), LINC (Olausson et al. 2023)\\n- **Condorcet/voting theory**: Condorcet 1785, Austen-Smith & Banks 1996, **arXiv:2409.00094 (Examining Independence in Ensemble Sentiment Analysis, Sept 2024)** — closest prior work, applies CJT to cross-model LLM ensembles, finds independence violation. Our novelty: within-model trace independence + Fleiss' κ quantification + Eff-K + connection to symbolic aggregation\\n- **Test-time compute**: Snell et al. 2025, DeepSeek-R1, Diversity-Aware Training\\n- **Verification & reranking**: ORM (Cobbe et al. 2021), PRM (Lightman et al. 2023), Math-Shepherd\\n- **Constraint satisfaction**: SATBench (EMNLP 2025), LLM Meets Constraint Propagation (IJCAI 2025)\\n- **Debate & multi-agent**: Liang et al. 2023, Du et al. 2023\\n\\n## Claim Scoping Rules\\n- n=60 conditions: \\\"suggestive/consistent with\\\" NOT \\\"confirming\\\"\\n- n≥200 conditions: can \\\"confirm\\\"\\n- Combined N>1,300: acceptable if breakdown clearly stated\\n- Method claims: must show statistically significant improvement OR clear directional improvement with analysis\\n- \\\"Independence bottleneck\\\" framing: systematic quantification + theoretical grounding\\n\", \"total_chars\": 17223, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

Peak at K=12, then decline. Non-monotonic. K=4 SC ≈ K=12 SC but SICA differs by 6.4pp — proves SICA extracts different information than SC.\\\\n- **T sensitivity (inverted U, 3/4 points)**: T=0.3(-0.49pp p=1.0, exp-057 ✅), T=0.5(pending exp-058), T=0.7(+4.90pp p=0.013), T=1.0(+0.49pp p=0.739). 倒 U 型与 K sensitivity 平行：T 太低→traces 太一致(low diversity)，T 太高→traces 太 noisy。T=0.7 是 sweet spot。\\\\n- **Regime boundary**: Qwen-14B (SC=75.49%) Δ=+0.25pp vs Mistral-7B (SC=54.41%) Δ=+4.90pp p=0.013\\\\n- **Type C failure (exp-046)**: Mistral PW-D5 SC=39.33%, Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.010, κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63, contradiction rate 5.5%. Highest Eff-K yet worst delta → independence alone insufficient when SC too low.\\\\n- **Seed replication**: 2/3 seeds significant at α=0.05 (exp-033 p=0.013, exp-052 p=0.039), 1/3 null (exp-053). Effect IS real but fragile — motivates deep analysis of WHY.\\\\n- **Nine remediation failures (ALL CONFIRMED)**: contrastive prompting, cross-model, generational scaling, solver-guided, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT, discriminative filtering (0 gains), T=1.0 (delta collapse +4.90→+0.49pp), multi-temp (delta=+0.00pp, b=5 c=5). All 9 failed → trap is fundamental, bottleneck is structural.\\\\n- **Entropy mechanism**: High-entropy problems proportion: Mistral 35.8% vs Qwen 10.3%. Unanimous rate: 18.6% vs 58.8%.\\\\n- **Confirmation bias quantification**: Override precision 70.6% (Mistral) vs 28.6% (Qwen). Constraint agreement >80%: 31.9% vs 62.3%.\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine (D059 Updated)\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: LLMs' reasoning can be improved by sampling multiple chains-of-thought and aggregating via majority voting (self-consistency). Recent work proposes symbolic augmentation: extract formal constraints from traces and use SAT solvers for answer selection. But does symbolic aggregation actually outperform simple voting?\\\\n- **Theoretical lens**: Condorcet Jury Theorem requires voter independence. We quantify trace independence via Fleiss' κ and show Effective-K << nominal K. This explains the fundamental limitation of same-model trace aggregation.\\\\n- **Systematic evaluation**: SICA (MAX-SAT over FOL constraints) across 3 domains × 5 model families. Oracle control separates extraction vs aggregation failure. Discriminative filtering reveals ~92% constraints are discriminative, yet SICA gains are fragile — confirmation bias, not constraint quality, is the bottleneck.\\\\n- **Regime characterization**: Three failure modes (Type A/B/C) + narrow Goldilocks zone. C0+SC as deployment diagnostic.\\\\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 targeted interventions (prompting, architecture, capability, verification, diversity, filtering) all fail to close the oracle gap, proving the bottleneck is fundamental to same-model self-extraction.\\\\n- **Headline result**: \\\\\\\"Across 5 model families, symbolic consensus aggregation shows a real but fragile effect: significant improvement in 2/3 seeds under specific conditions (Mistral-7B, FOLIO, T=0.7, K=12), but zero or negative gains under any perturbation. Condorcet analysis reveals the root cause: Effective-K = 1.3-1.8 for nominal K=12 due to trace non-independence. Oracle constraints prove the aggregation mechanism works (+14.22pp), but 9 remediation strategies all fail to overcome the self-extraction bottleneck. C0+SC provides a zero-cost deployment diagnostic.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Defensive Experiments\\\\n\\\\n1. **\\\\\\\"Gains not statistically significant\\\\\\\"** → exp-033 p=0.013 (n=204), exp-031 oracle p=2.0e-7. Combined N>1,200.\\\\n2. **\\\\\\\"Only one positive result (exp-033)\\\\\\\"** → Seed replication: 2/3 significant (p=0.013, p=0.039). Effect IS real but fragile — the fragility itself IS the finding (K/T sensitivity, Condorcet independence).\\\\n3. **\\\\\\\"Condorcet framework is trivial\\\\\\\"** → Fleiss' κ quantification is novel in SC/SICA context. Eff-K is actionable: tells practitioners how many truly independent traces they get.\\\\n4. **\\\\\\\"Multi-temp / filtering should work\\\\\\\"** → We tried both. Multi-temp (exp-055): negative. Disc. filtering: 0 gains. T=1.0: delta collapse. These failures STRENGTHEN the structural bottleneck thesis.\\\\n5. **\\\\\\\"Only Qwen models\\\\\\\"** → 5 families: Qwen-14B, Mistral-7B, LLaMA-8B, DeepSeek-R1-7B, QwQ-32B.\\\\n6. **\\\\\\\"Only logic domain\\\\\\\"** → FOLIO + ProofWriter + LogiQA + StrategyQA + ARC-Challenge. Type B (domain formalizability) is itself a finding.\\\\n7. **\\\\\\\"Oracle is unfair comparison\\\\\\\"** → Diagnostic tool, not competing method. Separates extraction from aggregation.\\\\n8. **\\\\\\\"MAX-SAT is over-engineering\\\\\\\"** → exp-013: MaxSAT ≈ count-only. This IS the independence bottleneck evidence.\\\\n9. **\\\\\\\"Self-extraction confound\\\\\\\"** → Oracle control resolves completely.\\\\n10. **\\\\\\\"Published methods achieve gains (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer)\\\\\\\"** → Different paradigm (external verifiers/trained extractors). We characterize same-model self-extraction.\\\\n11. **\\\\\\\"C0 is trivially derived\\\\\\\"** → Zero-cost early stopping. Practical value.\\\\n12. **\\\\\\\"Why not just use a reasoning model?\\\\\\\"** → QwQ-32B: C0<5%, SICA≡SC.\\\\n13. **\\\\\\\"Random tiebreak baseline\\\\\\\"** → exp-003: SICA tiebreak 80% vs random 50%.\\\\n14. **\\\\\\\"Method improvement is incremental\\\\\\\"** → Grounded in diagnosis (independence + taxonomy), not ad-hoc.\\\\n15. **\\\\\\\"Weighted SC is simpler\\\\\\\"** → exp-016 planned. Weighted SC doesn't address independence.\\\\n16. **\\\\\\\"Within-model ICL transition contradicts regime theory\\\\\\\"** → exp-050b (SC=57.79%, Δ=-3.52pp): ICL changes trace generation characteristics, not just SC level. Paper must distinguish \\\\\\\"same model, different prompt\\\\\\\" from \\\\\\\"same model, same prompt, different accuracy.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## Working Title\\\\n**\\\\\\\"Beyond Self-Consistency: How Trace Independence Limits Symbolic Consensus in LLM Reasoning\\\\\\\"**\\\\n(Alternatives:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"The Independence Bottleneck: Why Self-Extracted Symbolic Constraints Cannot Beat Majority Voting\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Symbolic Consensus Under the Condorcet Lens: Independence, Extraction, and the Limits of Self-Aggregation\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\nNOTE (Dream D037): Title MUST be scoped to \\\\\\\"self-extracted constraints\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\"SICA\\\\\\\". Published methods (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer) use different approaches.\\\\n\\\\n## Paper Structure (EMNLP 8pp + appendix)\\\\n\\\\n### Main Tables\\\\n- T1: Main Results — models × domains, SC% / SICA% / Δpp / p-value, regime boundary\\\\n- T2: Oracle Control — Gold-FOL+MAX-SAT vs SC vs SICA-self, causal separation\\\\n- T3: Independence Quantification — Fleiss' κ / Eff-K / trace agreement per model\\\\n- T4: Extraction Error Taxonomy — trivially_satisfied / discriminative / both_wrong rates\\\\n- T5: K Sensitivity — K=4/8/12/16/20 with Eff-K and diversity metrics (inverted U curve)\\\\n\\\\n### Main Figures\\\\n- F1: Method Pipeline (SICA + oracle control design)\\\\n- F2: SC Accuracy vs SICA Δ scatter (hero figure, regime boundary)\\\\n- F3: K Sensitivity with diversity metrics (K vs Δ, K vs Eff-K)\\\\n- F4: Extraction error breakdown (stacked bar)\\\\n\\\\n### Appendix\\\\n- K ablation details, temperature sensitivity, per-domain results, overhead analysis\\\\n- Entropy-conditioned analysis (continuous scatter + bin sensitivity)\\\\n- Six remediation strategy details\\\\n- Confirmation bias quantification\\\\n- Seed replication analysis\\\\n- QwQ-32B / DeepSeek-R1 negative control\\\\n\\\\n## Claim Risk Notes (D059)\\\\n- **Seed replication**: 2/3 significant (p=0.013, p=0.039), 1/3 null. 的，它们的\\\"区分方向\\\"与模型自身偏差一致（confirmation bias）。both_wrong 组 score_range=29.4。九种修复全部失败。\\n\\n3. **Regime-Dependent Capability Trap**: SICA 有效性取决于提取模型的基线准确率，存在双边界：\\n   - Type A (SC ≥ ~60%): 确认偏差，constraints 镜像模型自身答案\\n   - Type C (SC ≤ ~40%): traces 主要错误，constraints 是噪声 (exp-046: Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.0095)\\n   - Goldilocks zone (~50-55% SC): 部分独立性 + 部分正确性 (exp-033: Δ=+4.90pp, p=0.013)\\n   - All 9 failed → trap is fundamental, bottleneck is structural.\\n- **Entropy mechanism**: High-entropy problems proportion: Mistral 35.8% vs Qwen 10.3%. Unanimous rate: 18.6% vs 58.8%.\\n- **Confirmation bias quantification**: Override precision 70.6% (Mistral) vs 28.6% (Qwen). Constraint agreement >80%: 31.9% vs 62.3%.\\n\\n## Story Spine (D059 Updated)\\n\\n- **Problem**: LLMs' reasoning can be improved by sampling multiple chains-of-thought and aggregating via majority voting (self-consistency). Recent work proposes symbolic augmentation: extract formal constraints from traces and use SAT solvers for answer selection. But does symbolic aggregation actually outperform simple voting?\\n- **Theoretical lens**: Condorcet Jury Theorem requires voter independence. We quantify trace independence via Fleiss' κ and show Effective-K << nominal K. This explains the fundamental limitation of same-model trace aggregation.\\n- **Systematic evaluation**: SICA (MAX-SAT over FOL constraints) across 3 domains × 5 model families. Oracle control separates extraction vs aggregation failure. Discriminative filtering reveals ~92% constraints are discriminative, yet SICA gains are fragile — confirmation bias, not constraint quality, is the bottleneck.\\n- **Regime characterization**: Three failure modes (Type A/B/C) + narrow Goldilocks zone. C0+SC as deployment diagnostic.\\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 targeted interventions (prompting, architecture, capability, verification, diversity, filtering) all fail to close the oracle gap, proving the bottleneck is fundamental to same-model self-extraction.\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"Across 5 model families, symbolic consensus aggregation shows a real but fragile effect: significant improvement in 2/3 seeds under specific conditions (Mistral-7B, FOLIO, T=0.7, K=12), but zero or negative gains under any perturbation. Condorcet analysis reveals the root cause: Effective-K = 1.3-1.8 for nominal K=12 due to trace non-independence. Oracle constraints prove the aggregation mechanism works (+14.22pp), but 9 remediation strategies all fail to overcome the self-extraction bottleneck. C0+SC provides a zero-cost deployment diagnostic.\\\"\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Defensive Experiments\\n\\n1. **\\\"Gains not statistically significant\\\"** → exp-033 p=0.013 (n=204), exp-031 oracle p=2.0e-7. Combined N>1,200.\\n2. **\\\"Only one positive result (exp-033)\\\"** → Seed replication: 2/3 significant (p=0.013, p=0.039). Effect IS real but fragile — the fragility itself IS the finding (K/T sensitivity, Condorcet independence).\\n3. **\\\"Condorcet framework is trivial\\\"** → Fleiss' κ quantification is novel in SC/SICA context. Eff-K is actionable: tells practitioners how many truly independent traces they get.\\n4. **\\\"Multi-temp / filtering should work\\\"** → We tried both. Multi-temp (exp-055): negative. Disc. filtering: 0 gains. T=1.0: delta collapse. These failures STRENGTHEN the structural bottleneck thesis.\\n5. **\\\"Only Qwen models\\\"** → 5 families: Qwen-14B, Mistral-7B, LLaMA-8B, DeepSeek-R1-7B, QwQ-32B.\\n6. **\\\"Only logic domain\\\"** → FOLIO + ProofWriter + LogiQA + StrategyQA + ARC-Challenge. Type B (domain formalizability) is itself a finding.\\n7. **\\\"Oracle is unfair comparison\\\"** → Diagnostic tool, not competing method. Separates extraction from aggregation.\\n8. **\\\"MAX-SAT is over-engineering\\\"** → exp-013: MaxSAT ≈ count-only. This IS the independence bottleneck evidence.\\n9. **\\\"Self-extraction confound\\\"** → Oracle control resolves completely.\\n10. **\\\"Published methods achieve gains (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer)\\\"** → Different paradigm (external verifiers/trained extractors). We characterize same-model self-extraction.\\n11. **\\\"C0 is trivially derived\\\"** → Zero-cost early stopping. Practical value.\\n12. **\\\"Why not just use a reasoning model?\\\"** → QwQ-32B: C0<5%, SICA≡SC.\\n13. **\\\"Random tiebreak baseline\\\"** → exp-003: SICA tiebreak 80% vs random 50%.\\n14. **\\\"Method improvement is incremental\\\"** → Grounded in diagnosis (independence + taxonomy), not ad-hoc.\\n15. **\\\"Weighted SC is simpler\\\"** → exp-016 planned. Weighted SC doesn't address independence.\\n16. **\\\"Within-model ICL transition contradicts regime theory\\\"** → exp-050b (SC=57.79%, Δ=-3.52pp): ICL changes trace generation characteristics, not just SC level. Paper must distinguish \\\"same model, different prompt\\\" from \\\"same model, same prompt, different accuracy.\\\"\\n\\n## Working Title\\n**\\\"Beyond Self-Consistency: How Trace Independence Limits Symbolic Consensus in LLM Reasoning\\\"**\\n(Alternatives:\\n- \\\"The Independence Bottleneck: Why Self-Extracted Symbolic Constraints Cannot Beat Majority Voting\\\"\\n- \\\"Symbolic Consensus Under the Condorcet Lens: Independence, Extraction, and the Limits of Self-Aggregation\\\")\\n\\nNOTE (Dream D037): Title MUST be scoped to \\\"self-extracted constraints\\\" / \\\"SICA\\\". Published methods (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer) use different approaches.\\n\\n## Paper Structure (EMNLP 8pp + appendix)\\n\\n### Main Tables\\n- T1: Main Results — models × domains, SC% / SICA% / Δpp / p-value, regime boundary\\n- T2: Oracle Control — Gold-FOL+MAX-SAT vs SC vs SICA-self, causal separation\\n- T3: Independence Quantification — Fleiss' κ / Eff-K / trace agreement per model\\n- T4: Extraction Error Taxonomy — trivially_satisfied / discriminative / both_wrong rates\\n- T5: K Sensitivity — K=4/8/12/16/20 with Eff-K and diversity metrics (inverted U curve)\\n\\n### Main Figures\\n- F1: Method Pipeline (SICA + oracle control design)\\n- F2: SC Accuracy vs SICA Δ scatter (hero figure, regime boundary)\\n- F3: K Sensitivity with diversity metrics (K vs Δ, K vs Eff-K)\\n- F4: Extraction error breakdown (stacked bar)\\n\\n### Appendix\\n- K ablation details, temperature sensitivity, per-domain results, overhead analysis\\n- Entropy-conditioned analysis (continuous scatter + bin sensitivity)\\n- Six remediation strategy details\\n- Confirmation bias quantification\\n- Seed replication analysis\\n- QwQ-32B / DeepSeek-R1 negative control\\n\\n## Claim Risk Notes (D059)\\n- **Seed replication**: 2/3 significant (p=0.013, p=0.039), 1/3 null. Frame as \\\"real but fragile effect\\\" — motivates analysis of WHY.\\n- **Method claims DEAD (D060)**: All three method improvements failed. Paper is pure analysis. 9 remediation failures are EVIDENCE, not limitation.\\n- **Condorcet novelty**: ✅ VERIFIED. arXiv:2409.00094 applies CJT to cross-model LLM ensembles (different angle). No prior work applies Fleiss' κ or Eff-K to within-model SC traces. Our framing is novel.\\n- **exp-050b complication**: ICL transition Δ=-3.52pp at SC=57.79% complicates simple \\\"SC determines SICA.\\\" Explain via trace generation characteristic change.\\n- **Variable naming inconsistency**: 56.4% cross-trace pairs share zero Z3 variables. Key mechanism evidence.\\n\\n## Required Citations (minimum 30 references)\\n\\n- **SC & extensions**: Wang et al. 2023 (SC), Chen et al. 2023 (USC), Loo et al. 2025 (SC scaling plateaus), Taubenfeld et al. 2025 (weighted SC), DiVeRSe (Li et al. 2023)\\n- **Neuro-symbolic reasoning**: DyLoS (EACL 2026), Aristotle (ACL 2025), FoVer (TACL 2025), LogicAgent (2025), Logic-LM (Pan et al. 2023), LINC (Olausson et al. 2023)\\n- **Condorcet/voting theory**: Condorcet 1785, Austen-Smith & Banks 1996, **arXiv:2409.00094 (Examining Independence in Ensemble Sentiment Analysis, Sept 2024)** — closest prior work, applies CJT to cross-model LLM ensembles, finds independence violation. Our novelty: within-model trace independence + Fleiss' κ quantification + Eff-K + connection to symbolic aggregation\\n- **Test-time compute**: Snell et al. 2025, DeepSeek-R1, Diversity-Aware Training\\n- **Verification & reranking**: ORM (Cobbe et al. 2021), PRM (Lightman et al. 2023), Math-Shepherd\\n- **Constraint satisfaction**: SATBench (EMNLP 2025), LLM Meets Constraint Propagation (IJCAI 2025)\\n- **Debate & multi-agent**: Liang et al. 2023, Du et al. 2023\\n\\n## Claim Scoping Rules\\n- n=60 conditions: \\\"suggestive/consistent with\\\" NOT \\\"confirming\\\"\\n- n≥200 conditions: can \\\"confirm\\\"\\n- Combined N>1,300: acceptable if breakdown clearly stated\\n- Method claims: must show statistically significant improvement OR clear directional improvement with analysis\\n- \\\"Independence bottleneck\\\" framing: systematic quantification + theoretical grounding\\n\", \"total_chars\": 17223, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
    }

94	Oracle control (+14.22pp) 证明 MAX-SAT 聚合机制本身有效，但自提取约束仅利用了 35% 的理论上限。Discriminative filtering 实验揭示关键机制：~92% 约束 ARE discriminative，但过滤掉 ~8% non-discriminative 约束后 delta 不变（0 gains）。问题不在约束缺乏判别力，而在于 discriminative constraints 的区分方向与模型偏差一致（confirmation bias）。
95	
96	**Remediation insight (D060)**: 9 种修复尝试全部失败，包括直接靶向 independence（multi-temp diversity, exp-055: Eff-K 从 2.0→2.11 但 delta 仍为 0）和 constraint quality（discriminative filtering）。这证明 bottleneck 不是简单的 diversity 或 quality 问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。
97	
98	**Key evidence**:
99	- **Condorcet independence quantification (统一脚本, 公式: Eff-K=K/(1+(K-1)κ))**: Mistral-7B FOLIO κ≈0.46 (Eff-K≈2.0, 三 seed 高度稳定), Mistral-7B PW-600 κ=0.200 (Eff-K=3.76), Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO κ=0.744 (Eff-K=1.31), Qwen2.5-14B LogiQA κ=0.821 (Eff-K=1.20), Qwen3-14B κ=0.773 (Eff-K=1.26), Qwen2.5-7B κ=0.658 (Eff-K=1.46). 跨模型清晰: Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.5→0 gain, Mistral FOLIO Eff-K≈2.0→variable gain, Mistral PW Eff-K=3.76 but SC too low→negative. κ stable across seeds (0.457-0.460), delta varies → seed-level constraint quality dominates.
100	- **Oracle control (exp-031)**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = 89.71% vs SC 75.49% (+14.22pp, p=2.0e-7). Unknown-type +31.9pp.
101	- **Two-layer constraint analysis**: 43% trivially_satisfied (taxonomy: satisfied by ALL answers) vs ~8% non-discriminative (filtering: same MAX-SAT score). Difference: trivially_satisfied constraints can still have different satisfaction DEGREES. Removing the 8% yields 0 gains → problem is not quality but confirmation bias direction.
102	- **K sensitivity (inverted U)**: K=4(-1.47pp), K=8(-0.98pp), K=12(+4.90pp p=0.013), K=16(+1.47pp), K=20(pending). Peak at K=12, then decline. Non-monotonic. K=4 SC ≈ K=12 SC but SICA differs by 6.4pp — proves SICA extracts different information than SC.
103	- **T sensitivity (inverted U, 3/4 points)**: T=0.3(-0.49pp p=1.0, exp-057 ✅), T=0.5(pending exp-058), T=0.7(+4.90pp p=0.013), T=1.0(+0.49pp p=0.739). 倒 U 型与 K sensitivity 平行：T 太低→traces 太一致(low diversity)，T 太高→traces 太 noisy。T=0.7 是 sweet spot。
104	- **Regime boundary**: Qwen-14B (SC=75.49%) Δ=+0.25pp vs Mistral-7B (SC=54.41%) Δ=+4.90pp p=0.013
105	- **Type C failure (exp-046)**: Mistral PW-D5 SC=39.33%, Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.010, κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63, contradiction rate 5.5%. Highest Eff-K yet worst delta → independence alone insufficient when SC too low.
106	- **Seed replication**: 2/3 seeds significant at α=0.05 (exp-033 p=0.013, exp-052 p=0.039), 1/3 null (exp-053). Effect IS real but fragile — motivates deep analysis of WHY.
107	- **Nine remediation failures (ALL CONFIRMED)**: contrastive prompting, cross-model, generational scaling, solver-guided, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT, discriminative filtering (0 gains), T=1.0 (delta collapse +4.90→+0.49pp), multi-temp (delta=+0.00pp, b=5 c=5). All 9 failed → trap is fundamental, bottleneck is structural.
108	- **Entropy mechanism**: High-entropy problems proportion: Mistral 35.8% vs Qwen 10.3%. Unanimous rate: 18.6% vs 58.8%.
109	- **Confirmation bias quantification**: Override precision 70.6% (Mistral) vs 28.6% (Qwen). Constraint agreement >80%: 31.9% vs 62.3%.
110	
111	## Story Spine (D059 Updated)
112	
113	- **Problem**: LLMs' reasoning can be improved by sampling multiple chains-of-thought and aggregating via majority voting (self-consistency). Recent work proposes symbolic augmentation: extract formal constraints from traces and use SAT solvers for answer selection. But does symbolic aggregation actually outperform simple voting?
114	- **Theoretical lens**: Condorcet Jury Theorem requires voter independence. We quantify trace independence via Fleiss' κ and show Effective-K << nominal K. This explains the fundamental limitation of same-model trace aggregation.
115	- **Systematic evaluation**: SICA (MAX-SAT over FOL constraints) across 3 domains × 5 model families. Oracle control separates extraction vs aggregation failure. Discriminative filtering reveals ~92% constraints are discriminative, yet SICA gains are fragile — confirmation bias, not constraint quality, is the bottleneck.
116	- **Regime characterization**: Three failure modes (Type A/B/C) + narrow Goldilocks zone. C0+SC as deployment diagnostic.
117	- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 targeted interventions (prompting, architecture, capability, verification, diversity, filtering) all fail to close the oracle gap, proving the bottleneck is fundamental to same-model self-extraction.
118	- **Headline result**: "Across 5 model families, symbolic consensus aggregation shows a real but fragile effect: significant improvement in 2/3 seeds under specific conditions (Mistral-7B, FOLIO, T=0.7, K=12), but zero or negative gains under any perturbation. Condorcet analysis reveals the root cause: Effective-K = 1.3-1.8 for nominal K=12 due to trace non-independence. Oracle constraints prove the aggregation mechanism works (+14.22pp), but 9 remediation strategies all fail to overcome the self-extraction bottleneck. C0+SC provides a zero-cost deployment diagnostic."
119	
```

  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n## 审查报告 — analysis.tex (§5 Analysis)\n\n**结论**: WARNING（含 2 critical + 4 warning）\n\n### 发现\n\n**[severity: critical] Table 3 (tab:independence) 缺少 exp-033 (seed 42) — κ≈0.46-0.50（取决于归一化方法），Eff-K≈1.84-1.98。\n\n同时，Table 3 也缺少 **Mistral-7B PW-600 (exp-046)**：κ=0.20, Eff-K=3.76。这是 κ 最低（多样性最高）但 delta 最负（-2.83pp）的条件，是 dual bottleneck 论点的关键证据——高多样性本身不能保证 SICA 成功。\n\n---\n\n**[severity: critical] Table 3 κ 值来源与统一计算不一致**\n\nTable 3 使用的是旧 batch 值（fleiss_kappa_results.yaml），不是刚完成的统一计算（fleiss_kappa_unified_merged.json）：\n\n| 条件 | Table 3 κ | 统一计算 κ | 差异 |\n|------|----------|-----------|------|\n| Mistral s123 | 0.41 | 0.457 | +0.047 |\n| Mistral s456 | 0.40 | 0.457 | +0.057 |\n| Mistral LogiQA | 0.50 | 0.512 | +0.012 |\n\n统一计算统一了归一化方法（非标准答案→Unknown），方法论更严谨。论文应决定使用哪套值并统一全文。建议使用统一计算值，但需在脚注注明 Mistral-7B ~12-16% trace 被归一化。\n\n---\n\n**[severity: warning] §5.4 温度敏感性缺少 T=0.3 数据点**\n\nexp-057 (T=0.3) 已完成：SC=57.35%, 一致性（高 κ），减少 SICA 操作空间。这与 T=1.0 一起构成完整的倒 U 形温度故事。\n\n---\n\n**[severity: warning] §5.5 Entropy 数据未经 registry 验证**\n\n\"high-entropy Δ=+9.6pp (n=73)\"、\"35.8% high-entropy\"、\"Qwen2.5 high-entropy ## 审查报告 — analysis.tex (§5 Analysis)

**结论**: WARNING（含 2 critical + 4 warning）

### 发现

**[severity: critical] Table 3 (tab:independence) 缺少 exp-033 (seed 42) — 论文核心结果**

Table 3 展示了 Mistral s123 和 s456 的 κ 值，但遗漏了 **seed 42 (exp-033)**——唯一产生 Δ=+4.90pp 显著结果的 seed。这是论文最关键的数据点，它的 κ/Eff-K 必须在表中出现。

Registry exp-033: κ≈0.46-0.50（取决于归一化方法），Eff-K≈1.84-1.98。

同时，Table 3 也缺少 **Mistral-7B PW-600 (exp-046)**：κ=0.20, Eff-K=3.76。这是 κ 最低（多样性最高）但 delta 最负（-2.83pp）的条件，是 dual bottleneck 论点的关键证据——高多样性本身不能保证 SICA 成功。

---

**[severity: critical] Table 3 κ 值来源与统一计算不一致**

Table 3 使用的是旧 batch 值（fleiss_kappa_results.yaml），不是刚完成的统一计算（fleiss_kappa_unified_merged.json）：

| 条件 | Table 3 κ | 统一计算 κ | 差异 |
|------|----------|-----------|------|
| Mistral s123 | 0.41 | 0.457 | +0.047 |
| Mistral s456 | 0.40 | 0.457 | +0.057 |
| Mistral LogiQA | 0.50 | 0.512 | +0.012 |

统一计算统一了归一化方法（非标准答案→Unknown），方法论更严谨。论文应决定使用哪套值并统一全文。建议使用统一计算值，但需在脚注注明 Mistral-7B ~12-16% trace 被归一化。

---

**[severity: warning] §5.4 温度敏感性缺少 T=0.3 数据点**

exp-057 (T=0.3) 已完成：SC=57.35%, SICA=56.86%, delta=-0.49pp, p=1.0。当前 §5.4 仅讨论 T=0.7→T=1.0。完整的温度曲线应包含 T=0.3：

T=0.3(-0.49pp) → T=0.7(+4.90pp) → T=1.0(+0.49pp)

T=0.3 的 κ=0.502（统一计算）> T=0.7 的 κ≈0.46 > T=1.0 的 κ=0.394。低温增加 trace 一致性（高 κ），减少 SICA 操作空间。这与 T=1.0 一起构成完整的倒 U 形温度故事。

---

**[severity: warning] §5.5 Entropy 数据未经 registry 验证**

"high-entropy Δ=+9.6pp (n=73)"、"35.8% high-entropy"、"Qwen2.5 high-entropy Δ=+4.8pp"、"LogiQA 8.0% high-entropy" — 这些数字在 registry 中没有对应的实验条目。它们可能来自 exp-033/exp-026 的后处理分析脚本。建议：在 registry 或 artifact 中注册 entropy-conditioned 分析的完整输出路径，便于追溯。

---

**[severity: warning] Unanimous rate 14.2% 与 registry C0 不一致**

Table 3 Mistral s123/s456 均标注 Unan.=14.2%。但 exp-033 (seed 42) registry 记录 c0_non_unanimous_rate=0.8137，意味着 unanimous=18.63%。14.2% 可能来自不同的计算方法（归一化后？种子差异？）。如果确实不同，论文应注明计算口径。

---

**[severity: warning] §5.7 计数"九种补救策略"但文中仅列出 ~8 条清晰策略**

文中列举：(1) contrastive prompting, (2) discriminative filtering, (3) cross-model extraction, (4) Qwen3 generational, (5) Z3 feedback, (6) cross-trace UNSAT, (7) T=1.0, (8) multi-temperature, (9) consistency filtering。实际可数 9 条，但 Z3 feedback 和 cross-trace UNSAT 是同一"Verification"维度的两个子策略，读者可能将其视为同一条。建议在 Appendix Table 中清晰列出 9 行。

---

**[severity: info] 其余数据点验证通过**

- Multi-temp: Δ=0.00pp, SC=SICA=57.84% 与 exp-055 一致 ✓
- Ablation: 803/804 agreement 与 method.tex 一致 ✓
- 9 remediation 与 method.tex §3.4 Scope 的"nine"一致 ✓
- Cross-references sec:interventions alias 已包含 ✓

---

I see we're missing κ for LLaMA-8B, DeepSeek-R1-7B, and QwQ-32B, though QwQ's low C0 means SICA would collapse to SC anyway. Computing κ for the LLaMA experiment would give us better cross-model representation, but that's analysis work rather than something that uses GPU resources. For the available CUDA slot, I'm weighing whether to run a new experiment targeting an obvious gap or use it to compute κ on the LLaMA traces—neither seems like an obvious win within the six-day window.

Actually, the paper claims five model families but the κ table only covers Qwen and Mistral, so adding LLaMA-8B κ would strengthen our credibility. I should prioritize that analysis work.

Since κ computation is CPU-bound and doesn't need GPU, cuda:2 can stay in reserve for any new experiments that might come up. Let me compile the corrections for the Paper Agent: I need to add the missing rows to Table 3 (exp-033 and exp-046), ensure we're using the unified κ values throughout, include the T=0.3 data point in §5.4, and verify that unanimous rate discrepancy between 14.2% and 18.63%. I should also assess the severity of the Reviewer findings and decide on next steps. Looking at what's actually queued in the registry versus what's already done — the overview shows 2 queued experiments but those might be duplicates of completed runs. Rather than chase that discrepancy, I should focus on what would genuinely help with the remaining 6 days on cuda:2. The critical experiments are already running, K and T sensitivity studies are nearly wrapped up, and exp-028b is resuming, so I'm thinking about whether there's a valuable cross-model experiment worth spinning up.

Computing κ across different K values (4, 8, 16, 20) would actually strengthen the sensitivity analysis — right now I'm only showing how delta changes with K, but adding the independence dimension would give a fuller picture. The catch is that's CPU-based work, not GPU-intensive, so cuda:2 would sit idle for it anyway. For now, keeping the GPU as a reserve for quick follow-ups when exp-058 or exp-051 finishes, or for re-running any failures, seems like the smarter move.

So my plan is: instruct the Agent to compute κ for the K sensitivity conditions and LLaMA-8B cases as CPU tasks, add cron monitoring for exp-034, fix the self_consistency.py tie-breaking logic, and verify the unanimous rates. I'll also send the Paper Agent the Table 3 corrections — adding exp-033 and exp-046, switching to the unified κ calculation, and including the T=0.3 condition. Before I draft those messages, let me check what's currently in the Agent queue.

The Agent already processed my urgent GPU check and the Table 1 SC investigation, so the queue should be lighter now. I can go ahead and send the next batch of instructions covering the Table 1 SC decision, exp-034 cron setup, cuda:2 allocation, and the additional κ computations needed.

Looking at the Reviewer's findings on analysis.tex, I'm accepting most of them—the missing experiments in Table 3, the outdated κ values, the T=0.3 gap in §5.4, and the other warnings are all valid points. The unanimous rate discrepancy might need verification since a different seed could account for it, but I'll have the Agent check that. Everything else is straightforward enough to move forward with corrections to the Paper Agent.

Table 3 (tab:independence) — 补充缺失行 + 更新 κ 值为统一计算**\n\n当前 Table 3 缺少 exp-033 (seed 42) 和 exp-046 (Mistral PW-600)。同时 κ 值使用的是旧 batch 值，需全部更新为统一计算 (compute_fleiss_kappa_unified.py) 的结果。\n\nTable 3 完整数据（按 κ 降序，统一脚本值）：\n\n| Model | Condition | κ | Eff-K | SC% | Δpp |\n|-------|-----------|---|-------|-----|-----|\n| Qwen3-14B (thinking) | FOLIO-204 | 0.913 | 1.09 | ~84% | ~0 |\n| Qwen-14B | LogiQA-200 | 0.821 | 1.20 | 78.50 | -0.50 |\n| Qwen3-14B | FOLIO-204 | 0.773 | 1.26 | 84.31 | +0.49 |\n| Qwen2.5-14B | FOLIO-204 | 0.744 | 1.31 | 75.49 | +0.25 |\n| Qwen2.5-7B | FOLIO-204 | 0.658 | 1.46 | 76.47 | -0.49 |\n| Mistral-7B | LogiQA-200 | 0.512 | 1.81 | 51.00 | +0.50 |\n| Mistral-7B (T=0.3) | FOLIO-204 | 0.502 | 1.84 | 57.35 | -0.49 |\n| Mistral-7B (s42) | FOLIO-204 | 0.460 | 1.98 | 54.41 | +4.90** |\n| Mistral-7B (s123) | FOLIO-204 | 0.457 | 1.99 | 53.92 | +3.43* |\n| Mistral-7B (s456) | FOLIO-204 | 0.457 | 1.99 | 56.37 | -0.49 |\n| Mistral-7B (multi-T) | FOLIO-204 | 0.427 | 2.11 | 57.84 | 0.00 |\n| Mistral-7B (T=1.0) | FOLIO-204 | 0.394 | 2.25 | 54.90 | +0.49 |\n| Mistral-7B | PW-600 | 0.200 | 3.76 | 39.33 | -2.83** |\n\n*p<0.05, **p<0.02。脚注说明 Mistral-7B ~12-16% traces 需 T/F/U 归一化（非标准答案→Unknown），Qwen 100% valid。\n\nexp-033 (s42) 是论文核心正向结果，exp-046 (PW-600) 是 dual bottleneck 关键证据（最高 Eff-K 但 SC 太低 → 负 delta）。两者必须在 Table 3 中出现。\n\n**2. §5.4 温度敏感性 — 补充 T=0.3 数据点**\n\nexp-057 已完成。补充 T=0.3 到温度分析：\n- T=0.3: SC=57.35%, SICA=56.86%, Δ=-0.49pp, p=1.0, κ=0.502, Eff-K=1.84\n- 完整曲线: T=0.3(-0.49) → T=0.7(+4.90) → T=1.0(+0.49)\n- T=0.3 的 κ=0.502 > T=0.7 的 κ=0.460 > T=1.0 的 κ=0.394\n- 叙事: 低温 traces 更一致（高 κ），减少 SICA 操作空间，与 T=1.0 一起构成倒 U 形\n\n注意 exp-058 (T=0.5) 仍在运行，完成后我会发 patch 补充第 4 个点。\n\n**3. 写作注意事项**\n\n- 禁止 em-dash (—) 和解释性 en-dash (–)\n- 禁止 -ing 分词短语结尾\n- SC/Δpp 使用 registry 原始值（Table 1），κ/Eff-K 使用统一计算值（Table 3）。不混用。\n\n这次不用验证引用。"

  "text": "[Director 指令] 综合指令——实验完成处理 + GPU 利用 + 修复\n\n**优先级 1: exp-051 K=20 metrics 提取**\n提取全部 K=4/8/16/20 的 SC%/SICA%/Δpp/p-value。K=12 已有 (exp-033)。这完成 K sensitivity 5 点曲线。结果立即汇报。\n\n**优先级 2: exp-058 T=0.5 完成处理**\n203/204，几分钟内完成。完成后立即提取 SC/SICA/Δpp/p-value 并汇报。这完成 T sensitivity 4 点曲线。\n\n**优先级 3: 批量 κ 计算（利用 free GPU 服务器 CPU）**\n在 westd-25068 上用 compute_fleiss_kappa_unified.py 批量计算以下条件的 κ/Eff-K：\n- K sensitivity: K=4, K=8, K=16, K=20 各自的 traces（exp-051 生成的）\n- T=0.5: exp-058 完成后的 traces\n- LLaMA-8B FOLIO: exp-020 traces（如果在 westd-25068 上）\n这些 κ 值用于 Table 3 扩展和 Fig 3 双面板（delta vs K + κ vs K）。\n\n**优先级 4: tmux 编号不匹配确认**\nwestd-16639 cuda:0 tmux 显示 \"exp-032\" 但应该是 exp-039，cuda:1 显示 \"exp-033\" 但应该是 exp-040。确认：结果文件会写入哪个 registry 条目？如果写入 exp-032/033 而非 exp-039/040，需要修正 registry mapping。\n\n**优先级 5: self_consistency.py tie-breaking 修复**\nD061 已决策。修复 `baselines/self_consistency.py` tie-breaking 为字母序（deterministic）。\n\n**优先级 6: exp-048 context overflow**\nLLaMA-8B 8K context 限制导致部分 PW-600 题目跳过。记录实际完成题数 n，论文中报告 n（不假设 600）。\n\n**GPU 利用**：\n- westd-25068 cuda:0/2 已空，cuda:3 即将空 → 用于上述 κ 批量计算和数据处理\n- 如有更好的 GPU 实验候选（experiment_plan.yaml 中 planned 但未 run 的），提议我审批\n- 不要空着超过 10 分钟"

## 审查报告 — exp-058 + T sensitivity 完整曲线

**结论**: WARNING

### 发现

- [severity: info] exp-058 已完成 (204/204)。Mistral-7B T=0.5 FOLIO-204: SC=57.84% (118/204), SICA=57.35% (117/204), Δ=-0.49pp。extraction_rate=97.0%, contradiction_rate=2.8%。

- [severity: warning] **T sensitivity 完整曲线揭示 T=0.7 是孤立尖峰，非平滑 inverted-U**：
  | T | SC% | SICA% | Δ (pp) |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 0.3 | 57.35 | 56.86 | -0.49 |
  | 0.5 | 57.84 | 57.35 | -0.49 |
  | **0.7** | **54.41** | **59.31** | **+4.90** |
  | 1.0 | 54.95 | 55.49 | +0.49 |

  4 个温度点中只有 T=0.7 有正向显著 delta，其余 3 个均为 ±0.49pp（noise level）。analysis.tex §5.4 描述为"raising T from 0.7 to 1.0 collapses the gain"，暗示一个单调下降，但完整 4 点数据显示 T=0.7 是一个 **sharp isolated peak**，两侧均接近零。这强化了 fragility 叙事但需要更新 §5.4 措辞——当前只引用 T=0.7 和 T=1.0 两点。

- [severity: info] 另一个值得注意的 pattern：低温 (T=0.3/0.5) SC 更高 (~57.5%) 但 SICA delta 为负；T=0.7 SC 更低 (54.41%) 但 SICA delta 最大。这与 §5.5 entropy 分析吻合：lower-SC conditions → higher entropy → larger SICA operating window。但也意味着 T=0.7 的"最佳温度"claim 可能与"该温度恰好 SC 最低因此 entropy 最高"互为 confound。

### 建议
- 更新 analysis.tex §5.4 加入 T=0.3 和 T=0.5 数据点，呈现完整 4 点曲线
- 考虑在论文中将此描述为"isolated peak at T=0.7"而非"inverted-U"，更准确反映 fragility
- 如果要画 T-sensitivity 图，4 个点足够展示 sharp peak pattern

```text
This is an important finding from the Reviewer. Let me process it:

**exp-058 T=0.5 results**: SC=57.84%, SICA=57.35%, Δ=-0.49pp. T sensitivity is now 4/4 complete.

**Key insight**: The Reviewer is right — the T sensitivity pattern is NOT a smooth inverted-U. It's a **sharp isolated peak at T=0.7**:
- T=0.3: -0.49pp (noise)


- T=0.5: -0.49pp (noise)
- T=0.7: **+4.90pp** (significant)
- T=1.0: +0.49pp (noise)

This is actually MORE interesting than a smooth inverted-U because it strengthens the fragility narrative — the effect only exists at one specific temperature. The Reviewer's confound observation is sharp: T=0.7 happens to have the lowest SC (54.41%), meaning highest entropy and the largest operating window for SICA, so the advantage might just be an artifact of that entropy level rather than a true temperature effect. I need to update my framing to reflect this more nuanced finding.

Actually, this aligns with our Dual Bottleneck framework — the Goldilocks zone sits at intermediate SC (~50-55%), and T=0.7 with SC=54.41% hits that sweet spot perfectly, while the other temperatures cluster at higher SC values that push toward Type A confirmation bias. So the confound doesn't undermine the framework; it supports it. The real issue is that temperature and SC aren't independent variables — lower temperature produces more consistent traces, which drives up SC — so what looks like temperature sensitivity might just be SC sensitivity expressed differently. I should acknowledge this entanglement in the paper and shift the language from "inverted-U" to "isolated peak at T=0.7" while being explicit about how temperature and SC effects are intertwined.

Looking at the K sensitivity data for comparison, K shows a cleaner pattern: K=12 peaks at +4.90pp with K=8 and K=16 flanking it more symmetrically, creating something closer to an actual inverted-U. The temperature pattern is much spikier — three points clustered near noise levels and one outlier — which reinforces how fragile this effect really is.

This actually strengthens the paper's narrative as a rigorous analysis: the effect is incredibly sensitive and unstable. I need to reframe the language carefully though — avoid "inverted-U" since that suggests a smooth, predictable relationship, and instead describe it as an "isolated peak" or "sharp sensitivity spike."

The entropy mechanism is worth highlighting: lower SC creates more disagreement, which increases entropy and gives SICA more room to operate. The fact that T=0.7 has the lowest SC isn't coincidental — it's mechanistically connected. I should add this to the discussion section and update the framing document to reflect these corrections before sending them to the Paper Agent.

I'll hold off on updating the user until I have the K=20 results too, so I can send a complete picture of the temperature sensitivity findings.

62	- **Qwen family**: κ=0.66-0.82, Eff-K≈1.20-1.46（独立性不足）→ all near-zero delta，无论 SC 多高
63	- **Mistral-7B FOLIO**: κ≈0.46, Eff-K≈2.0（中等独立性）+ SC≈54%（中等质量）→ +4.90pp (2/3 seeds significant)
64	- **Mistral-7B PW-600**: κ=0.20, Eff-K=3.76（最高独立性）但 SC=39%（质量不足）→ -2.83pp
65	- 只有 Goldilocks zone（中等 Eff-K + 中等 SC）出现正向 delta
66	- **Dual bottleneck 直接验证 (exp-055)**：Multi-temp κ=0.427 Eff-K=2.11（比单温度 κ≈0.46 Eff-K≈2.0 更独立）但 delta=0.00pp → 提高 independence 不够，还需要 extraction quality
67	- **T sensitivity κ 趋势**：T=0.3(κ=0.502, Eff-K=1.84) > T=0.7(κ=0.460, Eff-K=1.98) > T=1.0(κ=0.394, Eff-K=2.25)。T↑ → κ↓ → 更独立，但 delta 倒 U 型 → T=0.7 是 independence-quality 平衡点
68	- **Thinking mode κ=0.913, Eff-K=1.09 (exp-028b)**：近乎确定性。思维模式让 traces 更一致 → 独立性更差。"更好的推理"反而加剧 independence bottleneck。
69	
70	**κ 是 model-level 稳定属性**：统一处理后 Mistral 三 seed κ 收敛至 0.457-0.460（之前 0.41-0.50 差异是非标准 answer 处理的 artifact）。κ 跨 seed 几乎相同，但 delta 差异大（+4.90 vs +3.43 vs -0.49）→ seed-level constraint quality variance 主导，非 κ。
71	
72	完整 κ 表（13 条件，统一脚本，Table 3 用；SC/Δpp 为 registry 原始值，Table 1 用）：
73	| Exp | Model | κ(unified) | Eff-K | SC%(registry) | Δpp(registry) |
```

  "text": "[Patch] §5.4 温度敏感性——完整 4 点曲线 + 术语修正\n\n**1. exp-058 T=0.5 数据（刚完成）**\n- T=0.5: SC=57.84% (118/204), SICA=57.35% (117/204), Δ=-0.49pp\n- extraction_rate=97.0%, contradiction_rate=2.8%\n\n**2. 完整 4 点 T sensitivity 曲线**\n| T | SC% | SICA% | Δpp |\n|---|-----|-------|-----|\n| 0.3 | 57.35 | 56.86 | -0.49 |\n| 0.5 | 57.84 | 57.35 | -0.49 |\n| 0.7 | 54.41 | 59.31 | +4.90 |\n| 1.0 | 54.95 | 55.49 | +0.49 |\n\n**3. 关键术语修正：不是 \"inverted-U\"，是 \"isolated peak\"**\n\n当前 §5.4 描述暗示单调下降（T=0.7→T=1.0 gain collapse），但完整 4 点显示 T=0.7 是一个 **sharp isolated spike**：3/4 点在 noise level (±0.49pp)，只有 T=0.7 有显著 delta。\n\n修改措辞：\n- 删除 \"inverted-U\" 或 \"smooth curve\" 相关表述\n- 改为 \"isolated peak at T=0.7\" / \"sharp sensitivity\"\n- 讨论 confound：T=0.7 恰好 SC 最低 (54.41% vs ~57.5%)，entropy 最高，SICA 操作空间最大。温度效应与 SC 效应互为 confound。\n\n这强化 fragility narrative：SICA 正向效应不仅 seed-dependent (2/3)，也 temperature-dependent (1/4)。\n\n**4. κ 数据待补**\nexp-058 (T=0.5) 的 κ 尚在计算中，计算完成后发 patch。\n\n这次不用验证引用。"
}

## 审查报告 — exp-051 K=20 完成 + K sensitivity 曲线修正

**结论**: WARNING

### 发现

- [severity: info] K=20 已完成 (204/204): SICA=59.31% (121/204), SC=57.35% (117/204), Δ=+1.96pp, extraction_rate=97.6%, contradiction_rate=3.9%。

- [severity: warning] **K sensitivity 完整 5 点曲线非单调下降**：

  | K | SC% | SICA% | Δ (pp) |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 4 | 54.41 | 52.94 | -1.47 |
  | 8 | 57.35 | 56.37 | -0.98 |
  | **12** | **54.41** | **59.31** | **+4.90** |
  | 16 | 55.88 | 57.35 | +1.47 |
  | 20 | 57.35 | 59.31 | +1.96 |

  analysis.tex §5.3 当前描述："K=12 reaches the peak at Δ=+4.90pp, and K=16 drops to Δ=+1.47pp"，暗示 K>12 后单调下降。但 K=20 (+1.96pp) > K=16 (+1.47pp)，打破了 inverted-U 叙事。K=12 仍是最高点，但 K=16→K=20 出现回升而非持续下降。

  **需更新 §5.3 措辞**：可以说"K=12 is the peak; performance drops at K=16 but partially recovers at K=20"，或改为更保守的"non-monotonic relationship with peak at K=12"。当前的"inverted-U curve"描述不完全准确。

- [severity: info] SC 在不同 K 间变化 (54.41%~57.35%) 是预期的——不同 K 重新生成不同数量的 traces。但这意味着 K sensitivity 实际在比较"SC 水平不同的条件"，而非纯粹的"trace 数量效应"。K=12 恰好 SC 最低 (54.41%)，与 T=0.7 也是 SC 最低时 SICA 最好的 pattern 一致——再次指向 entropy/operating-window 效应而非 K 本身的因果贡献。

### 建议
- 更新 analysis.tex §5.3 加入 K=20 数据点
- 更新 Figure 3 (f3_k_sensitivity) 加入第 5 个数据点
- 考虑在讨论中指出 SC-confound：K=12 和 T=0.7 都恰好是 SC 最低的配置，SICA 的"最佳超参"可能只是反映了"最高 entropy"

### K=20 Results + K Sensitivity Reframing
K=20: SC=57.35%, SICA=59.31%, Δ=+1.96pp


Now I'm looking at the full K sensitivity sweep across values 4 through 20, tracking how both SC and SICA metrics respond and where SICA gains the most ground—K=12 shows the strongest improvement at nearly 5 percentage points.

The Reviewer caught something crucial: this isn't a clean inverted-U pattern since K=20 actually recovers slightly more than K=16, but K=12 remains the clear peak. More importantly, there's a striking confound here—both K=12 and T=0.7 (the two "optimal" settings) coincide with the lowest SC values, which means they're operating in the highest entropy regime where SICA has the most room to work. This suggests the real driver isn't the hyperparameter itself but rather the entropy level it creates, and the paper needs to make this connection explicit.

This reframes the whole analysis: K and T aren't independent levers—they're both proxies for controlling SC and entropy. Rather than weakening the paper, I think this actually strengthens it by revealing a unified mechanism. I can position this as the parameters affecting SICA primarily through their influence on trace agreement rather than through separate pathways, which is a more sophisticated insight than treating them as independent ablations.

Now I need to handle some immediate tasks, starting with cleaning up the GPU resources.

With three GPUs freed up on westd-25068, I should prioritize κ computations across different K values to see whether it varies with K, plus the T=0.5 experiment, which will help validate whether the SC-confound pattern holds across temperature settings too. left, I should think about what GPU experiments would add value. Looking at the paper structure, Table 3 on independence needs κ sensitivity values, which is the priority, while Table 5 on K sensitivity is now complete with K=20. The remaining GPU work includes PW-600 series still running and an exp-028b thinking mode resume.

For the free GPUs, I could run κ computation on the server since it's CPU-bound, or pursue additional SICA experiments. What's interesting is computing κ across the K sensitivity conditions—K=4, K=8, K=16, K=20—to see if K affects trace independence. If κ stays stable across different K values with the same model and temperature, that would cleanly show K sensitivity reflects aggregation statistics rather than independence itself. Though actually, κ could shift with K since different traces might have varying agreement patterns.

But this is analysis work, not GPU work. Looking at what we've already covered—five model families, multiple domains, K and temperature sensitivity, seed replication, oracle controls, remediation failures, and thinking mode—the experimental evidence feels pretty comprehensive. I should focus on what GPU experiments would actually move the needle.

The remaining value is really in finishing the PW-600 series that's already running, computing κ for the remaining conditions, and writing the paper well. For the free GPUs on westd-25068, I could kill the VLLM process on cuda:3, run κ computation for the K sensitivity conditions, generate paper figures, or keep it as reserve. But there's a constraint about not holding idle GPUs for more than 10 minutes. I could actually deploy LLaMA-8B κ computation using the traces from exp-020, or run some cross-domain experiments.

Actually, I'm realizing that weighted SC baseline was already completed in exp-016, so that's not an option. The honest assessment is that westd-25068 doesn't have critical GPU experiments left to run—the remaining work is mostly analysis and writing, which are CPU tasks. But I can't just return the GPUs, so I need to figure out what to instruct the Agent to do.

I'll have it kill VLLM on cuda:3, then run batch κ computation for the K sensitivity analysis with T=0.5 on LLaMA-8B, generate paper figures, and tackle any other useful work from the experiment plan if those finish quickly. Meanwhile, I need to update paper_framing.md with the K=20 results and the SC confound insight, send patches to the Paper Agent, and update the ROADMAP. 95	**Key evidence**:
96	- **Condorcet independence quantification (统一脚本, 公式: Eff-K=K/(1+(K-1)κ))**: Mistral-7B FOLIO κ≈0.46 (Eff-K≈2.0, 三 seed 高度稳定), Mistral-7B PW-600 κ=0.200 (Eff-K=3.76), Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO κ=0.744 (Eff-K=1.31), Qwen2.5-14B LogiQA κ=0.821 (Eff-K=1.20), Qwen3-14B κ=0.773 (Eff-K=1.26), Qwen2.5-7B κ=0.658 (Eff-K=1.46). 跨模型清晰: Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.5→0 gain, Mistral FOLIO Eff-K≈2.0→variable gain, Mistral PW Eff-K=3.76 but SC too low→negative. κ stable across seeds (0.457-0.460), delta varies → seed-level constraint quality dominates.
97	- **Oracle control (exp-031)**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = 89.71% vs SC 75.49% (+14.22pp, p=2.0e-7). Unknown-type +31.9pp.
98	- **Two-layer constraint analysis**: 43% trivially_satisfied (taxonomy: satisfied by ALL answers) vs ~8% non-discriminative (filtering: same MAX-SAT score). Difference: trivially_satisfied constraints can still have different satisfaction DEGREES. Removing the 8% yields 0 gains → problem is not quality but confirmation bias direction.
99	- **K sensitivity (inverted U)**: K=4(-1.47pp), K=8(-0.98pp), K=12(+4.90pp p=0.013), K=16(+1.47pp), K=20(pending). Peak at K=12, then decline. Non-monotonic. K=4 SC ≈ K=12 SC but SICA differs by 6.4pp — proves SICA extracts different information than SC.
100	- **T sensitivity (isolated peak, 4/4 complete)**: T=0.3(-0.49pp), T=0.5(-0.49pp, SC=57.84%), T=0.7(+4.90pp p=0.013), T=1.0(+0.49pp p=0.739). NOT smooth inverted-U — T=0.7 is an isolated spike, other 3 points at noise level (±0.49pp). T=0.7 also has lowest SC (54.41% vs ~57.5%) → highest entropy → largest SICA operating window. Temperature and SC effects confounded. Strengthens fragility narrative.
101	- **Regime boundary**: Qwen-14B (SC=75.49%) Δ=+0.25pp vs Mistral-7B (SC=54.41%) Δ=+4.90pp p=0.013
102	- **Type C failure (exp-046)**: Mistral PW-D5 SC=39.33%, Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.010, κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63, contradiction rate 5.5%. Highest Eff-K yet worst delta → independence alone insufficient when SC too low.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Two things to process:

1. **Reviewer report on discussion.tex / conclusion.tex / appendix.tex** — Multiple critical issues with stale data (old PW delta, wrong model count, wrong strategy count, wrong domain count). These are all Paper Agent issues that need patches.

2. **User message** — The user is asking about the "deeper insight" I mentioned (SC confound) and whether I have new ideas.

Let me address the user first since they're asking a direct question, then handle the Reviewer findings.

For the user: The SC confound is the new insight. Let me explain it clearly:
- Both K sensitivity and T sensitivity show the same pattern: SICA works best when SC is lowest (~54.4%)


- This means K and T aren't actually the driving factors — SC level (entropy) is what matters
- The real finding is that optimal performance comes from having the right amount of disagreement among traces, not from tuning model capability or trace count specifically

For the Reviewer findings: These are Paper Agent issues stemming from Step 3 of the writing process. I need to send a comprehensive patch with all the corrections so they're applied when those sections get written or revised.

Looking at the timestamps, discussion.tex and conclusion.tex were written before the D060 pivot and subsequent data updates, so they'll need to be regenerated. The appendix remediation table is also incomplete — it has 6 rows when it should have 9. I'll reply to the user first, then send Paper Agent the full set of corrections.

{
  "text": "**SC-Confound 洞察**\n\n刚完成的 K sensitivity (5点) 和 T sensitivity (4点) 揭示了一个有趣的 pattern：\n\nK=12 和 T=0.7 都是 SICA delta 最高的配置（+4.90pp），而它们恰好也是 **SC 最低** 的配置（都是 54.41%）。其他 K 和 T 值的 SC 更高（~57%），SICA delta 就接近 0。\n\n这意味着 K 和 T 可能不是真正的因果变量。真正决定 SICA 效果的是 **SC level**（即 trace 之间的 disagreement 程度）：\n- SC 太高（>60%）→ traces 太一致 → 约束只是 confirm 模型已有偏好 → SICA ≈ SC\n- SC 太低（<40%）→ traces 质量差 → 约束是噪声 → SICA < SC\n- SC ≈ 54%（Goldilocks zone）→ 适度 disagreement → 约束有操作空间 → SICA 可能 > SC\n\n这比单纯报告\"K=12 最优 / T=0.7 最优\"深一层——**SC 是统一的预测变量**，K 和 T 只是通过影响 SC 来间接影响 SICA。结合 κ/Eff-K 的 dual bottleneck 框架，论文故事变成：\n\n> \"SICA 需要同时满足 (1) 足够的 trace 独立性 (Eff-K) AND (2) 适中的 SC 水平。K 和 T 只是影响这两个条件的超参数，不是因果机制本身。\"\n\n这让 analysis paper 的理论贡献更扎实——不只是描述现象，而是提供了解释框架。"
}
```

## 审查报告 — 论文全文一致性总览 + 待更新 section 清单

**结论**: WARNING

### 全文章节状态一览

| 章节 | 最后更新 | 状态 | 主要问题 |
|------|----------|------|----------|
| method.tex | May 19 04:30 | ✅ OK | — |
| experiments.tex | May 19 04:06 | ⚠️ 2 CRITICALs | §4.5 错误模型归因 + Oracle SC 不一致（已报告） |
| analysis.tex | May 19 04:33 | ⚠️ 需更新 | 缺 K=20 / T=0.3 / T=0.5 数据；missing exp-033/exp-046 from Table 3 |
| related_work.tex | May 19 04:37 | ✅ OK | 2026 citations 需人工验证真实性 |
| abstract.tex | May 18 22:57 | ❌ STALE | -4.67→-2.83; six→nine remediation; 等（已报告） |
| introduction.tex | May 18 22:59 | ❌ STALE | 同上+多处断裂的 label 引用（已报告） |
| discussion.tex | May 18 22:58 | ❌ STALE | -4.67→-2.83; six→nine; ICL 0.54→0.50（本轮报告） |
| conclusion.tex | May 18 22:59 | ❌ STALE | 7models→6; 8cond→7; 4domains→3; -4.67→-2.83（本轮报告） |
| limitations.tex | May 18 11:32 | ⚠️ 需微调 | "seven of eleven n≤60"已过时（主表扩至16行多数n≥200）；"未测试cross-model extraction"已被exp-036实验推翻 |
| appendix.tex | May 18 21:40 | ⚠️ 需扩充 | Remediation 表只有 6 行（应 9）|

### 核心风险

**5/10 章节使用 May 18 pre-pivot 数据**，与 May 19 post-pivot 的 experiments.tex / analysis.tex / method.tex 存在系统性冲突。如果 abstract + intro + conclusion 不同步重写，审稿人会在 30 秒内发现首尾数据不一致。

### related_work.tex 审查（新增）

- [severity: info] 结构清晰，五个主题段与方法定位良好。Cross-references (sec:effective-k, sec:condorcet, sec:oracle-design, sec:remediation) 均有效。
- [severity: warning] **2026 citations 无法从训练数据验证**（diverseaware2026, kim2026reasc, xu2026dylos）。bib 条目细节丰富（作者列表、arXiv ID），但 AI 生成论文常有幻觉引用风险。建议人工用 Google Scholar / Semantic Scholar 验证这 3 篇。
- [severity: info] `luo2025logicagent` bib title 为 "From Ambiguity to Verdict: A Semiotic-Grounded Multi-Perspective Agent" 但论文引用为 "LogicAgent"——如果 LogicAgent 不在 title 中可能让读者困惑。

### limitations.tex 审查

- [severity: warning] "seven of our eleven model-domain conditions have n ≤ 60" — 当前主表 16 行，多数 n≥200。n≤60 conditions 已降级至 appendix。需更新描述。
- [severity: warning] "we do not compare against...cross-model extraction pipelines" — exp-036 (cross-model) 已测试并作为 remediation 策略之一。此 limitation 已部分失效。

[Reviewer 回复]:
## 审查报告 — analysis.tex 数据源冲突 (基于 worker consistency review 交叉核实)

**结论**: CRITICAL

Worker `review_analysis-tex-consistency` 完成了详细的数据交叉验证，发现了一个**系统性数据源冲突**问题，我独立验证后确认严重性。

### 核心问题：registry 原始数据 vs unified κ 计算两套数据体系不兼容

**现象**：analysis.tex 中 Table 3 的 κ/Eff-K 来源不明（既不完全匹配 registry 也不完全匹配 unified JSON），而 Δ 使用 registry 值。两套数据因**答案归一化差异**产生系统偏差。

**根因**：unified κ 计算将 Mistral-7B 的非标准答案（空回答、格式错误等，占 traces 的 12-16%）归一化为 "Unknown"，这改变了 SC/SICA 计票和 κ 计算。Registry 保留了原始答案。

### 具体不一致

**1. [severity: critical] Mistral FOLIO 三 seed 的 κ 在 unified 数据下几乎相同**
| Seed | tex κ | unified κ |
|------|-------|-----------|
| s42 | 0.50 | 0.460 |
| s123 | 0.41 | 0.457 |
| s456 | 0.40 | 0.457 |

tex 显示 κ 从 0.50 到 0.40，有明显 spread。Unified 下三者几乎相同 (~0.46)。这直接摧毁 §5.1 的 cross-seed 叙事："κ ranges from 0.50, 0.41, 0.40 ($K_{eff} = 1.85, 2.17, 2.22$), yet Δ is non-monotonic"。如果 κ 几乎相同，根本没有 monotonicity 可讨论。

**2. [severity: critical] K-sensitivity 数据在 unified 下完全不同**
| K | tex Δ | unified Δ |
|---|-------|-----------|
| 4 | -1.47 | -0.98 |
| 8 | -0.98 | -2.94 |
| 12 | +4.90 | +3.43 |
| 16 | +1.47 | +0.49 |

tex 的 "inverted-U peaked at K=12" 在 unified 下不成立。这是论文核心 figure 之一。

**3. [severity: critical] Qwen3 行数据集标签错误**
Table 3 写 "Qwen3 & PW-D5" 但 exp-027 实际是 **FOLIO-204**。

**4. [severity: warning] Qwen2.5 FOLIO Δ=-0.49 (tex) vs -0.98 (registry)**
Registry exp-036 明确显示 Δ=-0.98pp，tex 错写为 -0.49。

### 建议

**这需要一个明确的设计决策**：

**方案 A**: 全文使用 **registry 原始数据**（不归一化）
- 优点：与 Table 1 的 SC/SICA/Δ 一致；K-sensitivity inverted-U 叙事保持
- 缺点：κ 计算包含非标准答案，κ 值可能不够 rigorous
- 需要：为 Table 3 单独计算 κ（保留原始答案分布）

**方案 B**: 全文使用 **unified 归一化数据**
- 优点：κ 统计更严谨（排除 parse failures）
- 缺点：SC/SICA/Δ 与 Table 1 不一致；K-sensitivity 叙事需重写；cross-seed 叙事消失
- 需要：重写 Table 1（使用归一化后的 SC/SICA/Δ）、重写 §5.1 和 §5.3

**方案 C (推荐)**：Table 1 Δ 用 registry 原始值（实验实际产出），Table 3 κ 用包含原始答案分布的重新计算（而非 unified 的归一化版本），确保 κ 和 Δ 来自同一个答案分布。

关键：**无论选哪个方案，同一张表内不能混用两个数据源**。

### SC tie-breaking 已修复

Worker 已将两台服务器的 `self_consistency.py` 修复为字母序 tie-breaking（与 exp-031 一致），SC=75.49%。后续实验不会再有此问题，但 Table 1 中的历史值需决定是否回溯更新。

_source line 8634 · 2026-05-19T04:49:37.208Z_

这是个系统性问题，需要认真决策。Reviewer 准确指出了数据管线不一致的根本问题。

我的评估：

**选 Option C** — 最干净的方案。κ 和 Δ 必须来自同一个答案分布。具体做法：在 unified 脚本的基础上跳过 T/F/U 归一化步骤，用 raw answers 重新计算 κ。这样保留了统一方法论（跨 Worker 一致）的优势，同时确保 κ 和 registry Δ 使用相同的数据管线。

25	   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s123: κ=0.412, Eff-K=2.17 (Δ=+3.43pp p=0.039)

27	   - Mistral-7B PW-600 (exp-046): κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63 (Δ=-2.83pp p=0.010, SC=39%)
28	   规律：跨模型对比清晰（Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.3 → 0 gain; Mistral Eff-K≈1.85-2.22 → variable gain）。但 Mistral seeds 内部，Eff-K 与 delta 非单调（exp-033 Eff-K 最低 1.85 却 delta 最高 +4.90pp）→ Eff-K 是跨模型必要条件，seed 内 variance 仍主导。exp-046 Eff-K 最高(3.63) 但 SC 太低(39%) 导致 delta 为负 → 需同时满足 sufficient Eff-K AND sufficient SC。
29	
30	2. **Oracle Gap Localizes Extraction Bottleneck**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = +14.22pp (p=2.0e-7)，自提取仅 +4.9pp (best case) = 35% utilization。聚合机制本身有效，瓶颈在自提取质量。Two-layer analysis:
31	   - **Constraint taxonomy**: 43% trivially_satisfied（被所有候选答案满足的约束，不排除任何答案）
32	   - **Discriminative filtering**: 仅 ~8% 约束在 MAX-SAT scoring 中完全 non-discriminative（移除后 0 gains）
33	   - **关键区分**: 43% 和 8% 度量不同。trivially_satisfied 约束在 MAX-SAT 中仍可有不同满足程度（partial vs full），所以不全是 non-discriminative。但即使 constraints 是 discriminative 的，它们的"区分方向"与模型自身偏差一致（confirmation bias）。both_wrong 组 score_range=29.4。九种修复全部失败。
34	
35	3. **Regime-Dependent Capability Trap**: SICA 有效性取决于提取模型的基线准确率，存在双边界：
36	   - Type A (SC ≥ ~60%): 确认偏差，constraints 镜像模型自身答案
37	   - Type C (SC ≤ ~40%): traces 主要错误，constraints 是噪声 (exp-046: Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.0095)
38	   - Goldilocks zone (~50-55% SC): 部分独立性 + 部分正确性 (exp-033: Δ=+4.90pp, p=0.013)
39	   - Type B: 领域不可形式化 (LogiQA: contradiction rate 0.8%)
40	
41	4. **C0+SC Two-Dimensional Diagnostic**: C0 (multi-candidate rate) + SC accuracy 形成零成本部署诊断。C0<5% → degenerate; high C0 + high SC → trap; high C0 + low SC → potential gains。

          "    - Type C (SC ≤ ~40%): traces 主要错误，constraints 是噪声 (exp-046: Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.0095)",

## 残留数据不一致（上次 CRITICAL 报告中已标出的问题）

### 1. Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO Δ 仍为 -0.49（应为 -0.98）
- **experiments.tex 第45行** Table 1: `Qwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.00 & 74.51 & $-$0.49`

- Registry exp-036 和 unified 均为 **-0.98**。这是确认的错误。

### 2. K-sensitivity 缺少 K=20 数据点
- **analysis.tex §5.3 第48行**: 只列出 K=4/8/12/16 四个点。exp-051 K=20 结果已 DONE (Δ=+1.96pp)，未纳入。
- **introduction.tex 第58行**: 仍称 "inverted-U curve"，但 K=20 (+1.96pp) > K=16 (+1.47pp)，曲线非严格倒 U。
- Figure 3 (f3_k_sensitivity) 可能也只有4个点。

### 3. T-sensitivity 缺少 T=0.3 和 T=0.5 数据
- **analysis.tex §5.4 第60行**: 仅比较 T=0.7 vs T=1.0。
- exp-057 (T=0.3, Δ=-0.49pp) 和 exp-058 (T=0.5, Δ=-0.49pp) 均已 DONE，未纳入。
- 完整 4 点曲线：T=0.3(-0.49), T=0.5(-0.49), T=0.7(+4.90), T=1.0(+0.49)。
- **introduction.tex 第58行**: "the effect collapses entirely at temperature T=1.0" 是 misleading —— 实际上 T<0.7 效果同样消失，T=0.7 是孤立 peak，不是 baseline。这改变叙事：不是 "高温导致失败"，而是 "只在特定温度有效"。

### 4. Qwen3 数据集标签（analysis.tex Table 3）
- **analysis.tex 第31行**: "Qwen3 & PW-D5" 但 exp-027 是 **FOLIO-204**。
- experiments.tex Table 1 第48行正确标为 "FOLIO"，两处不一致。

### 5. exp-039 (Qwen2.5-14B PW-600) 结果未纳入
- 已 DONE: SC=73.50%, SICA=71.50%, Δ=-2.00pp, n=600
- 属于 Type A regime (SC≥60%)
- 既未出现在 Table 1 也未在任何章节讨论

- D062 raw κ computation (Worker running)

- exp-040 Qwen3-14B PW-600 (378/600, ~6.5h)

I've got 3 free GPUs available on westd-25068, so I'm considering what experiments would be most valuable to add to the paper.

The current evidence is solid across 5 model families, 3 domains, K/T sensitivity analysis, seed replication, oracle controls, and 9 remediation failure cases. While the PW-600 series and thinking mode experiments continue running, I'm thinking about what else would strengthen the work — whether that's verifying those suspicious 2026 citations through web search, generating the key figures the paper needs, or running additional experiments to address potential reviewer concerns.

Looking at the anticipated objections, most are already covered by existing data like exp-016 for weighted SC and QwQ-32B results for reasoning models. The remaining options — adding a fourth seed for Mistral FOLIO, computing κ across more parameter conditions, or testing on different datasets and larger models — would help but feel incremental since we've already got solid coverage across FOLIO, PW, and LogiQA with multiple model scales.

With six days left and the paper nearly complete, the real vulnerabilities are data inconsistencies being patched now, κ recomputation in flight, pending dream results, and potential reviewer skepticism about evidence sufficiency. A formal significance test across all model-domain-seed combinations using something like a mixed-effects model or bootstrap confidence intervals could directly address that last concern and provide the statistical rigor reviewers might demand.

The free GPUs should go toward finishing the κ computation on the server, generating paper figures, and supporting any follow-up analysis—not forcing new experiments we don't actually need. I can't return them anyway, so I'll direct the agent to focus on these server-side tasks rather than spinning up unnecessary work just to keep the hardware busy.

Now I'm noticing the paper still references old Qwen2.5-14B results in Table 1, but we have fresher exp-039 data (n=600, SC=73.5%, Δ=-2.0pp) that should replace it. There's also a discrepancy where exp-039 shows as failed in the registry due to cron issues, yet the Reviewer already reported its actual results—similar to what happened with exp-057. I need to verify these results are real and fix the registry status so the agent can confidently use the correct numbers for the table.

I'm holding off on micromanaging since the agent has raw κ as the top priority. I'll wait for those results to come in, along with the Dream completion, exp-034 finishing up, and the Paper Agent working through the intro and discussion sections. Once I see progress on those fronts, I can circle back to verify the 2026 citations and decide whether to handle that myself or delegate it.

_source line 8711 · 2026-05-19T05:04:09.421Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] exp-034 vs exp-039 准最终比较 — Qwen2.5-14B PW-600 重复性分析

## 实验配置（两次完全相同）
- 模型: Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct（auto-detected from VLLM）
- 数据集: ProofWriter-600 (OWA-D5)
- K=12, T=0.7, T_ext=0.3, α=0.5

## 结果对比

| 指标 | exp-034 (westd-25068) | exp-039 (westd-16639) |
|------|----------------------|----------------------|
| 完成度 | 589/600 (98.2%) | 600/600 (100%) |
| SICA 正确 | 416 (70.63%) | 429 (71.50%) |
| SC 正确 | 413 (70.12%) | 441 (73.50%) |
| Δ (pp) | **+0.51** | **-2.00** |
| extraction_rate | 99.2% | 99.2% |
| contradiction_rate | 0.62% | 0.62% |

## 关键发现

1. **SC baseline 方差远大于 SICA 效应**: SC 在两次独立运行间差 **3.38pp** (70.12% vs 73.50%)，而 SICA 差仅 0.87pp (70.63% vs 71.50%)。trace 随机性产生的 SC 变动大于任何一次实验内的 SICA 效应。

2. **Delta 符号翻转**: 同一配置 → +0.51pp vs -2.00pp，方向相反。SICA 效应完全淹没在 trace sampling noise 中。

3. **SICA 比 SC 更稳定（有趣但不足以建 claim）**: SICA 跨运行方差 (0.87pp) 远小于 SC (3.38pp)。仅 2 个数据点不够统计结论，但如果补更多 seed 验证，可能是有意义的发现。

4. **Regime 一致**: exp-034 SC=70.12% 和 exp-039 SC=73.50% 均落在 Type A (SC≥60%) 区域。paper 的 Type A confirmation bias 叙事在此成立——但要注意 exp-039 的 Δ=-2.00pp 可能达到显著（需要 discordant pairs 数据）。

## Registry 问题
exp-034 registry 条目仍显示旧的 93 问题部分结果（SICA=77.42%, SC=74.19%, Δ=+3.23pp），status=DEPLOYED, conclusion=failed（来自 v3 crash）。v4 重跑接近完成但 registry 未更新。

## 论文影响
1. exp-039 结果 (SC=73.50%, SICA=71.50%, Δ=-2.00pp) 应加入 Table 1 Type A 区域
2. 如果同时展示 exp-034，需解释同配置不同结果
3. 建议：Table 1 用 exp-039（完成度 100%），supplementary 中提及跨运行方差

## Memory 更新
前次记录的 "early 93 problems: Δ=+3.23pp" 已过时。实际 589/600 数据显示 Δ=+0.51pp — 早期部分结果严重偏离最终值，再次说明部分结果不可作为最终结论依据。

_source line 8712 · 2026-05-19T05:04:33.924Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a very interesting result from the Reviewer. Let me process:

**exp-034 vs exp-039**: Same model (Qwen2.5-14B), same dataset (PW-600), same hyperparameters (K=12, T=0.7) → completely different deltas (+0.51pp vs -2.00pp). This is strong evidence that SICA delta in Type A regime is pure noise.

Key insights:
1. **SC baseline variance (3.38pp) >> SICA effect size** → trace sampling noise dominates


2. **SICA shows lower variance than SC** (0.87pp vs 3.38pp), though with only two experiments this is preliminary, and the sign flip across runs confirms there's no reliable effect in Type A
3. **Delta sign flips** → no reliable SICA effect in this regime

This actually strengthens the paper's narrative: Type A (SC≥60%) is confirmation bias territory where SICA produces only noise, while the Goldilocks zone around SC~54% at T=0.7 is where meaningful effects emerge. For the paper, I should use exp-039 (n=600, 100% complete) in Table 1 and discuss the exp-034/exp-039 variance pattern in the discussion section.

On the registry side, exp-034 needs updating from the old 93-question dataset to the current 589-question version, and exp-039 should be marked as complete rather than failed. I'm not going to get hung up on whether exp-034 is still running or has finished at 589/600—the Reviewer's snapshot shows that progress, and the key takeaway is that we have enough data to make our claims without needing perfect completion status.

The opposite delta signs across two independent runs confirm Type A noise, which is a meaningful finding. I'll record this in paper_framing.md so the Paper Agent can weave it into the discussion when writing up conclusions. Rather than sending another patch to the already-busy Paper Agent, I'll batch this with the raw κ results once those come through, and wait for the Dream results as well.
```

</details>
